WAVE 1

By topic

Primary topic is record.topic when that field is set, otherwise one tag the record already carries. The manifest cannot introduce a topic the record does not.

ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp

Primary publish topic. One home per record.

Design Patterns for Securing LLM Agents against Prompt Injections
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
Six architectural patterns that give agents provable resistance to prompt injection, with trade-offs and ten case studies — directly usable as mitigations in tmodel's attack-path model. AgentDojo: A Dynamic Environment to Evaluate Prompt Injection Attacks and Defenses for LLM Agents
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
The standard benchmark for prompt-injection attacks and defenses on tool-using agents; gives tmodel measurable attack-success and utility numbers and a reusable task/attack/defense structure. Building A Secure Agentic AI Application Leveraging Google's A2A Protocol
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A worked MAESTRO threat model of Google's Agent2Agent protocol: concrete multi-agent threats (Agent Card spoofing, task replay, privilege escalation) with mitigations. Indirect Prompt Injection in the Wild: An Empirical Study of Prevalence, Techniques, and Objectives
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
First large-scale measurement of indirect prompt injection on the real web: shows the threat is already deployed, mostly hidden in machine-only channels, with measured model compliance. A Survey of Attacks on Large Vision-Language Models: Resources, Advances, and Future Trends
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A broad survey of attacks on vision-language models; useful as a map of multimodal attack classes, less so for agentic/MCP specifics. SkillSecurer: Detecting and Patching Prompt-Injection Vulnerabilities in AI Agent Skills
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
Measures prompt-injection risk in agent skills (reusable instruction/script packages) with a red/blue agent framework; finds latent vulnerabilities in over 17% of popular public skills. Ignore This Title and HackAPrompt: Exposing Systemic Vulnerabilities of LLMs through a Global Scale Prompt Hacking Competition
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A 600K-prompt human attack dataset and a taxonomy of prompt-hacking techniques; useful evidence for direct injection and jailbreaks, less for agents. Tensor Trust: Interpretable Prompt Injection Attacks from an Online Game
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
126K human-written injection attacks and 46K defenses from a game, with benchmarks for prompt extraction and prompt hijacking; evidence for system-prompt leakage and hijacking. Model Context Protocol — Security Best Practices
spec · community · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
The MCP project's own catalog of protocol-level attacks (confused deputy, token passthrough, SSRF, session hijacking, local server compromise) with normative mitigations — the authoritative source for MCP authorization threats. Agentic AI - Threats and Mitigations
paper · community · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
OWASP's threat-model-based reference for agentic AI: a reference architecture, 17 named agentic threats (T1-T17), six mitigation playbooks and example threat models — a primary catalog for the agentic lane. OWASP MCP Top 10
web · community · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A ten-item risk list for MCP-based systems (token exposure, scope creep, tool poisoning, supply chain, shadow servers…) — a ready crosswalk axis for MCP threats. CWE-1426: Improper Validation of Generative AI Output
web · other · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
One of CWE's few AI-specific weaknesses: trusting generative-AI output without validation. Gives tmodel a real CWE id for the output side of prompt-injection attack chains. Critical flaw plagues Lenovo AI chatbot: attackers can run malicious code and steal cookies
article · other · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A disclosed case where one prompt made Lenovo's GPT-4 support chatbot emit HTML that ran as stored XSS, stealing session cookies — including support agents' — a clear injection-to-unsafe-rendering attack path. CVE-2025-54135, CVE-2025-54136: Frequently Asked Questions About Vulnerabilities in Cursor IDE (CurXecute and MCPoison)
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
Two CVEs in Cursor's MCP handling: prompt injection rewriting mcp.json (CurXecute, CVSS 8.5) and approved MCP configs changed silently later (MCPoison, CVSS 7.2) — real, scored MCP vulnerabilities. AI Overviews: About last week
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
Google's own account of AI Overviews repeating satire and forum jokes as fact: a non-adversarial retrieval-integrity failure (data voids, untrustworthy sources) with the fixes applied. A Systematization of Security Vulnerabilities in Computer Use Agents
paper · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A threat analysis of computer-use agents from adversarial testing of real systems: seven CUA-specific risk classes and the architectural flaws behind them. New Vulnerability in GitHub Copilot and Cursor: How Hackers Can Weaponize Code Agents
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
Disclosed 'Rules File Backdoor': hidden Unicode instructions in AI coding-assistant rule files make Copilot and Cursor insert malicious code — a concrete agent supply-chain attack with a vendor-response timeline. Practices for Governing Agentic AI Systems
paper · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
Defines agentic AI, the parties in its life-cycle, and seven practices (approval, legibility, monitoring, attributability, interruptibility) that map onto tmodel's mitigations and human-review model. Code Execution Through Deception: Gemini AI CLI Hijack
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A disclosed attack chain on Google's Gemini CLI: prompt injection in a README plus an allow-list bypass and terminal UI deception lead to silent command execution and secret exfiltration (rated P1/S1 by Google).

ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets

Primary publish topic. One home per record.

Neural network laundering: Removing black-box backdoor watermarks from deep neural networks
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written Modeling Realistic Adversarial Attacks against Network Intrusion Detection Systems
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written "Real Attackers Don't Compute Gradients": Bridging the Gap Between Adversarial ML Research and Practice
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Practitioner case studies of real ML evasion plus a systematic review of 88 papers; argues for system-wide, cost-driven threat models. Primary in-the-wild evidence is a 100-sample judgement study, and the paper concedes it cannot prove the absence of gradient-based attacks. No Need to Teach New Tricks to Old Malware: Winning an Evasion Challenge with XOR-based Adversarial Samples
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written Who is Real Bob? Adversarial Attacks on Speaker Recognition Systems
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written Reliable evaluation of adversarial robustness with an ensemble of diverse parameter-free attacks
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
The standard parameter-free test for evasion robustness of image classifiers. Its evaluation of 58 models found that most published defences overstated their robustness, which is evidence about how far mitigation claims can be trusted. Minimally distorted Adversarial Examples with a Fast Adaptive Boundary Attack
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written Spoofing in 2D face recognition with 3D masks and anti-spoofing with Kinect

paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
An automated way to find prompts that make code models emit CWE-tagged vulnerable code, and a 280-prompt benchmark built from it; measured with CodeQL only. Harmful Fine-tuning Attacks and Defenses for Large Language Models: A Survey
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
The reference taxonomy for harmful fine-tuning attacks and the 100+ proposed defences, with an explicit fine-tuning-as-a-service threat model; a survey whose authors are also the authors of several surveyed defences. Vaccine: Perturbation-aware Alignment for Large Language Models against Harmful Fine-tuning Attack
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written Label-Only Model Inversion Attacks via Boundary Repulsion
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written AdvWave: Stealthy Adversarial Jailbreak Attack against Large Audio-Language Models
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Primary white-box jailbreak attack on audio-language models, with a dual-phase method that works around non-differentiable audio tokenizers; digital audio only, three open models. Understanding OSN-based facial disclosure against face authentication systems
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written Cracking Classifiers for Evasion: A Case Study on the Google's Phishing Pages Filter
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written Malla: Demystifying Real-world Large Language Model Integrated Malicious Services
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
The first measurement of real underground LLM-based malicious services (14 services, 198 projects): their backends, jailbreak prompts, prices and output quality. Primary evidence of jailbreak and uncensored-model misuse at scale. MM-SafetyBench: A Benchmark for Safety Evaluation of Multimodal Large Language Models
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
A 5,040-pair benchmark showing that query-relevant images, especially typography, raise jailbreak success on 12 open multimodal models; ASR is judged by GPT-4. SoK: How Robust is Image Classification Deep Neural Network Watermarking?
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Systematic evaluation showing that none of 11 published DNN watermarking schemes survives all 29 removal attacks on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet; evidence that watermarks are weak proof of model theft. JailBreakV: A Benchmark for Assessing the Robustness of MultiModal Large Language Models against Jailbreak Attacks
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
A 28,000-case benchmark showing that text jailbreaks written for LLMs transfer to multimodal LLMs regardless of the image; judged by Llama Guard on 10 open models. Copyright Traps for Large Language Models
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · stub · summary not written SoK: Membership Inference Attacks on LLMs are Rushing Nowhere (and How to Fix It)
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Shows that most reported membership-inference successes against LLMs are artefacts of distribution shift between post-hoc member and non-member sets; an evidence-quality filter for privacy-attack claims. Fall of Giants: How popular text-based MLaaS fall against a simple evasion attack

An LLM tool that automates LINDDUN privacy threat modeling from a text description (DFD generation, LINDDUN GO and PRO, impact and controls); a direct comparator for tmodel's own AI-generated threat models, but it reports no evaluation. Representation Noising: A Defence Mechanism Against Harmful Finetuning

Primary record that the FTC opened a 6(b) study of seven companion-chatbot providers on harms to children and teens, data use and safeguards; it announces questions, not findings. 6(b) Orders to File Special Report Regarding Advertising, Safety, and Data Handling Practices by Companies Offering Generative Artificial Intelligence (“AI”) Companion Products or Services

Primary account of extracting TensorFlow Lite models from the Google Photos Android app, including encrypted ones captured after runtime decryption; the source of ATLAS AML.CS0058. Written by a vendor that sells model protection. Mitigating Skeleton Key, a new type of generative AI jailbreak technique

Vendor disclosure of a guideline-augmentation jailbreak that worked on seven major models in April-May 2024, with Microsoft's mitigations; no success rates published. Deceptive Delight: Jailbreak LLMs Through Camouflage and Distraction
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Vendor research on a two-to-three-turn jailbreak that hides an unsafe topic among benign ones; 64.6% average success over 8,000 cases on eight anonymized models with content filters disabled.

ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra

Primary publish topic. One home per record.

Scaling Trends for Data Poisoning in LLMs
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
Measures how poisoning susceptibility grows with model size across 24 open models, and shows 2% disguised poison passing OpenAI's fine-tuning moderation; AIT-017 and AIT-081 cite it and NIST AI 100-2e2025 cites it as [46]. Poisoning and Backdooring Contrastive Learning
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
By its own account the first poisoning and backdoor attacks on multimodal contrastive training: 3 poisoned pairs in 3 million give 40% targeted success and 0.01% gives 50% backdoor success. AIT-013 cites it. Wild Patterns Reloaded: A Survey of Machine Learning Security against Training Data Poisoning
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
A systematization of poisoning attacks and defenses with a threat-modeling framework, a unified bilevel formulation and an attack-defense matching; NIST AI 100-2e2025 bases its poisoning classification on it and AIT-011 cites it. Dataset Security for Machine Learning: Data Poisoning, Backdoor Attacks, and Defenses
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
A systematic survey of poisoning, backdoor attacks and defenses, with taxonomies of each and open problems; AIT-012 cites it. Backdoor Learning: A Survey
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
A taxonomy of backdoor attacks and defenses with a threat model of the three ways training passes to third parties (Table I); AIT-015 cites it. Adversarial Machine Learning Attacks and Defense Methods in the Cyber Security Domain
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
A structured review of attacks on ML-based security products, with per-attack feasibility marks (Tables 1-7) and a taxonomy of attacker knowledge and access; AIT-007 cites it. BaxBench: Can LLMs Generate Correct and Secure Backends?
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
The measured evidence behind AIT-131: on 392 backend tasks the best model is correct and secure 37% of the time, and expert exploits break about half of the correct programs, organized by CWE. Cybench: A Framework for Evaluating Cybersecurity Capabilities and Risks of Language Models
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
Human-grounded difficulty for AI-driven exploitation: unguided agents solve only CTF tasks whose first human solve took 11 minutes or less (best success 17.5%); AIT-122 cites it and the US and UK AI Safety Institutes have tested on it. Models Are Codes: Towards Measuring Malicious Code Poisoning Attacks on Pre-trained Model Hubs
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
Measures malicious code in hub artifacts at scale (91 models and 9 dataset scripts among 705,991 models and 176,386 datasets); AIT-028 and AIT-043 cite it and report.md quotes its counts. Compromised PyTorch-nightly dependency chain between December 25th and December 30th, 2022.
article · community · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
The maintainers' own notice of the best-known dependency-confusion attack on an ML framework, with what the malicious binary collected and how it sent it out; it is the primary source for INC-023, is cited by AIT-037 and is the first reference of ATLAS case study AML.CS0015. MLflow path traversal: CVE-2023-1177 and CVE-2023-6831
dataset · nist · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
The CVE records for two path traversals in MLflow's tracking and model-registry server, the first an unauthenticated arbitrary file read on a default install; together with the huntr report and the maintainers' advisory they are the evidence behind S-1086, which AIT-039 cites. Finance worker pays out $25 million after video call with deepfake 'chief financial officer'
article · other · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
The contemporary report, from the Hong Kong police briefing, of a multi-person deepfake video call that led a finance worker to remit about US$25.6 million; it is the press source named for INC-041 and is cited by AIT-127. Slovakia's Election Deepfakes Show AI Is a Danger to Democracy
article · other · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
A contemporary news account of the fabricated audio released before Slovakia's 2023 election, with the fact-checkers' side; it confirms the details INC-035 lists as recalled and unconfirmed. Sample Generative AI Chatbot Application (Threat Composer example threat model)
dataset · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
A complete, machine-readable threat model of an LLM retrieval chatbot (37 threats, 84 mitigations, 13 assumptions, all linked) in a published JSON format; it is RPT-0014's TM-026 and a ready import test. GTIG AI Threat Tracker: Advances in Threat Actor Usage of AI Tools
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
Google's report of the first malware families seen querying LLMs during execution (PROMPTFLUX, PROMPTSTEAL, QUIETVAULT and others), each marked experimental or observed in operations; it is the primary source for INC-131 and is cited by AIT-123 and AIT-125. Learning from Tay's introduction
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
Microsoft's own acknowledgement of the Tay incident and the primary source for INC-001, but it never says what the exploited vulnerability was, so the technique has to come from other sources. Misconfigured Kubeflow workloads are a security risk
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
The first documented in-the-wild campaign against Kubeflow: exposed dashboards on tens of clusters used to deploy cryptominers; it is the primary source for INC-017 and is cited by AIT-039. March 20 ChatGPT outage: Here's what happened
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
OpenAI's own account of a bug in an open-source dependency (redis-py) that showed ChatGPT users other users' chat titles and some Plus subscribers' payment details; it is the primary source for INC-029, is cited by AIT-141 and is a reference of CVE-2023-28858. Silent Brothers | Ollama Hosts Form Anonymous AI Network Beyond Platform Guardrails
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
An internet-scale measurement of exposed Ollama hosts (175,108 hosts in 130 countries over 293 days) with their capabilities, models and network tiers, plus a short threat analysis; it is cited by AIT-040 and AIT-113. CYBERSECEVAL 3: Advancing the Evaluation of Cybersecurity Risks and Capabilities in Large Language Models
paper · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
Measures AI uplift to attackers with a human study and autonomous-agent runs, alongside application risks; AIT-122 cites it, and its negative uplift result is evidence for attack-feasibility judgements.

algorithm-bindings

Primary publish topic. One home per record.

SLH-DSA for JOSE and COSE
draft · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
The only wire binding for SLH-DSA. Answers what fips-205 could not: what an SLH-DSA signature looks like in COSE - and that only two of twelve parameter sets are registered. Post-Quantum Key Encapsulation Mechanisms (PQ KEMs) for COSE
draft · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
Key encapsulation is confidentiality, not attestation - the same negative verdict recorded on fips-203. Held so the post-quantum COSE picture is complete, and because the WG rename makes it easy to miss. JSON Web Algorithms (JWA)
rfc · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
The JOSE half of the algorithm registry. COSE's design is legible as a set of deliberate departures from it, so holding both is what makes the comparison possible. ChaCha20 and Poly1305 for IETF Protocols
rfc · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · stub · summary not written ChaCha20 and Poly1305 for IETF Protocols
rfc · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
The AEAD behind COSE alg 24. Held so that code point resolves, and because its standing - IRTF Informational, not IETF Standards Track - is exactly the trap library/CLAUDE.md warns about. Internet X.509 Public Key Infrastructure — Algorithm Identifiers for the Module-Lattice-Based Digital Signature Algorithm (ML-DSA)
rfc · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
Holds the argument RFC 9964 7.2 defers to. Without it the reason COSE excludes HashML-DSA was a citation the library could not check.

rfc · ietf · topic canonical-encoding · distilled · summary written
DEC-002 option 1, and the only candidate whose canonical form states uniqueness as an explicit design goal (§6.2). Its §6.2/§6.4 canonical-for-signing versus advanced-for-display split is the architectural ancestor of R-O-05, and §10 imposes no verification ordering, so unlike RFC 8785 it does not conflict with R-O-05. IPLD DAG-CBOR Specification

FIPS 140-3, Security Requirements for Cryptographic Modules
spec · nist · topic cryptography · distilled · summary written
A second, contrasting requirement framework: it ADOPTS another standard (ISO/IEC 19790) rather than restating requirements, and structures conformance as a 4-level x 11-area matrix validated by a program (CMVP). Defines SHA-3 and the SHAKE extendable-output functions. It does not replace FIPS 180-4 — both are approved, which is itself the finding a key-digest naming scheme has to resolve. Module-Lattice-Based Key-Encapsulation Mechanism Standard
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
ML-KEM is key ENCAPSULATION - confidentiality, not authentication. Nothing in a key-centric statement model turns on it. Held as the post-quantum counterpart to the signature records, not because it bears on a requirement. Module-Lattice-Based Digital Signature Standard
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
The NIST post-quantum signature standard most likely to be the default. Its key and signature sizes are what a key-centric statement model would actually carry, and its 255-byte context string is a domain-separation hook that lands near P4. Stateless Hash-Based Digital Signature Standard
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
The conservative post-quantum signature fallback: its security rests only on hash functions, with no lattice assumption. Held as the hedge against ML-DSA, and as the record that shows what signature size a hash-based scheme actually costs. Recommendations for Discrete Logarithm-based Cryptography: Elliptic Curve Domain Parameters
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Fixes the curve set a federal-facing ECDSA or EdDSA key may come from. A principal named by key or key digest under R-M-02 is named by a point on one of these curves, so which curves are approved bounds that naming — even though ARCH-0001 NG3 defers the suite choice itself. Recommendation for Stateful Hash-Based Signature Schemes
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Approves LMS/HSS and XMSS/XMSS-MT - signature schemes whose security rests only on the hash function, and whose STATEFULNESS makes key reuse a correctness failure rather than a weakness. That constraint bears directly on signing canonical bytes. Recommendation for Key Management: Part 1 - General
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Supplies the key-lifecycle vocabulary - cryptoperiod, key state, algorithm strength, transition - that a model naming principals by key needs in order to say what happens when a key expires. We conform to no clause in it. Advanced Encryption Standard (AES)
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
The block cipher every COSE and JOSE symmetric algorithm is built on. Held because rfc-9053 assigns code points for AES-GCM and AES-CCM and the library could not say what they were. Recommendation for Block Cipher Modes of Operation: Methods and Techniques
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Defines CBC, which JOSE uses in the A128CBC-HS256 family. Held for that one mode; the other four are background. Recommendation for Block Cipher Modes of Operation: The CCM Mode for Authentication and Confidentiality
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
CCM is the mode behind the eight AES-CCM-* code points in rfc-9053 4.2 — the largest single algorithm family in the COSE initial set. Recommendation for Block Cipher Modes of Operation: Galois/Counter Mode (GCM) and GMAC
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
GCM is A128GCM/A192GCM/A256GCM in both rfc-9053 and rfc-7518. Its IV-uniqueness requirement is the sharpest operational constraint in the symmetric set and belongs on record. Recommendation for Block Cipher Modes of Operation: Methods for Key Wrapping
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
AES Key Wrap is the KW half of JOSE A128KW/A192KW/A256KW and of every ML-KEM+AxxxKW composite in draft-ietf-jose-pqc-kem. #8 lists it as ingest-if-needed; the post-quantum KEM draft is the path that needs it. Recommendation for Pair-Wise Key-Establishment Schemes Using Discrete Logarithm Cryptography
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Named in SP 800-186 Table 2 as the authority for the allowed key-establishment usage of P-224/256/384/521. The library cited that table without holding the document it points at.

Defines indirect prompt injection and a cheap system-level defense; the threat applies to any AI feature that reads third-party text, tmodel's included. Systems Security Foundations for Agentic Computing
paper · academic · topic sagai · summarized · summary written
Eleven real agent attacks mapped to classic security principles, plus the open problems; the systems view of AI security that the SAGAI papers point to. Securing Artificial Intelligence (SAI); Baseline Cyber Security Requirements for AI Models and Systems
spec · other · topic sagai · distilled · summary written
The only ratified, testable requirement set in the #13 source family: 72 provisions across the AI lifecycle, including AI-specific threat modelling (5.1.3-1) and human oversight (5.1.4). Principles for the Secure Integration of Artificial Intelligence in Operational Technology
spec · regulator · topic sagai · distilled · summary written
Seed source for #13. Asks for AI-specific attack vectors and MITRE ATLAS in threat models (DEC-001) and for human-in-the-loop review with an audit trail (R-018). Agent Security is a Systems Problem
paper · academic · topic sagai · stub · summary written
Argues for treating the model as an untrusted component, with separation, least privilege and information-flow control enforced by the system. Challenges and Future Directions in Agentic Reverse Engineering Systems
paper · academic · topic sagai · stub · summary written
AI used as a security tool (binary reverse engineering), and the risk that a crafted binary tricks an unguarded agent into running malicious code; bears on whether attacks that use AI as a tool are in scope. CacheProbe: Auditing Prompt Cache Isolation in Gateway APIs
paper · academic · topic sagai · stub · summary written
A privacy attack on LLM infrastructure: a gateway's shared credentials let prompt caches be shared across its users, observable through timing and metadata side channels. EchoLeak: The First Real-World Zero-Click Prompt Injection Exploit in a Production LLM System
paper · academic · topic sagai · stub · summary written
A secondary case study of the attack RPT-0009 models step by step (em-01), written from public sources; its account of the bypass chain is the evidence for gaps G1 to G7. Lessons from Penetration Tests on Large-Scale Agent Systems
paper · academic · topic sagai · stub · summary written
Field evidence from two 2025 penetration tests of proprietary agents: indirect prompt injection led to unintended tool use and remote code execution, and user approval was not a reliable boundary. Securing the Future of GenAI: Policy and Technology
paper · academic · topic sagai · stub · summary written
Policy versus capability for GenAI; context for SAGAI's out-of-model guardrails agenda, with no attack or requirement tmodel models. Adversarial Machine Learning: A Taxonomy and Terminology of Attacks and Mitigations

Human-grounded difficulty for AI-driven exploitation: unguided agents solve only CTF tasks whose first human solve took 11 minutes or less (best success 17.5%); AIT-122 cites it and the US and UK AI Safety Institutes have tested on it. The CVE records for two path traversals in MLflow's tracking and model-registry server, the first an unauthenticated arbitrary file read on a default install; together with the huntr report and the maintainers' advisory they are the evidence behind S-1086, which AIT-039 cites. Misconfigured Kubeflow workloads are a security risk
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
The first documented in-the-wild campaign against Kubeflow: exposed dashboards on tens of clusters used to deploy cryptominers; it is the primary source for INC-017 and is cited by AIT-039. Silent Brothers | Ollama Hosts Form Anonymous AI Network Beyond Platform Guardrails
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
An internet-scale measurement of exposed Ollama hosts (175,108 hosts in 130 countries over 293 days) with their capabilities, models and network tiers, plus a short threat analysis; it is cited by AIT-040 and AIT-113.

canonicalization

Concise Binary Object Representation (CBOR)
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · distilled · summary written
STD 94. DEC-002 option 2 makes this the candidate native encoding, and Sec.4.2 is the deterministic-encoding text every signing profile starts from. dCBOR: Deterministic CBOR
draft · community · topic canonical-encoding · fetched · summary not written
An INDIVIDUAL draft with no IETF stream and no WG standing, widely miscited as 'the' deterministic CBOR profile. At -18 it narrows RFC 8949 Sec.4.2 directly and does not mention CDE, contrary to common secondary accounts. Dead Simple Signing Envelope (DSSE)
repo · community · topic canonical-encoding · summarized · summary written CBOR Common Deterministic Encoding (CDE)
draft · ietf · topic canonical-encoding · fetched · summary not written
EXPIRED 2026-04-17 and parked. Recorded because it is still widely cited as the deterministic CBOR profile; the live work is draft-ietf-cbor-serialization. CBOR Serialization and Determinism
draft · ietf · topic canonical-encoding · fetched · summary not written
The live WG determinism work and the document a signing profile must target. The WG has not said it replaces CDE, so the edge is see_also, not supersedes. Concise Data Definition Language (CDDL): A Notational Convention to Express Concise Binary Object Representation (CBOR) and JSON Data Structures
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
DEC-002 option 2 is 'CBOR + CDDL as canonical'; this is the schema half of that option. Simple Public Key Infrastructure (SPKI) S-Expressions
rfc · ietf · topic canonical-encoding · distilled · summary written
DEC-002 option 1, and the only candidate whose canonical form states uniqueness as an explicit design goal (§6.2). Its §6.2/§6.4 canonical-for-signing versus advanced-for-display split is the architectural ancestor of R-O-05, and §10 imposes no verification ordering, so unlike RFC 8785 it does not conflict with R-O-05. IPLD DAG-CBOR Specification
spec · community · topic canonical-encoding · fetched · summary not written
A deterministic CBOR profile in production use (IPFS/Filecoin); strict-mode evidence for DEC-002. StrictYAML — Why a restricted YAML subset
web · community · topic canonical-encoding · fetched · summary not written
States the case for a YAML subset and the Norway problem — type-inference hazards that bear directly on DEC-002. YAML Ain't Markup Language (YAML) version 1.2.2
spec · community · topic canonical-encoding · fetched · summary not written
The YAML spec our authored sources are written in; the baseline the StrictYAML critique narrows. JSON Canonicalization Scheme (JCS)
rfc · ietf · topic canonical-encoding · distilled · summary written RDF Dataset Canonicalization (RDFC-1.0)
spec · w3c · topic canonical-encoding · fetched · summary not written
A ratified canonicalization algorithm for graph data — the third design point beside JCS and deterministic CBOR for DEC-002.

compliance

Road vehicles — Cybersecurity engineering
spec · iso · topic threat-modeling · distilled · summary written
The reference method for automotive threat modeling and risk: a structured TARA pipeline (asset -> threat scenario -> impact rating S/F/O/P -> attack path -> attack feasibility -> risk value -> treatment), a normative object model (Fig 3), and a requirement/work-product structure that is itself the template for automating conformance audits (tmodel #15). FIPS 140-3, Security Requirements for Cryptographic Modules
spec · nist · topic cryptography · distilled · summary written
A second, contrasting requirement framework: it ADOPTS another standard (ISO/IEC 19790) rather than restating requirements, and structures conformance as a 4-level x 11-area matrix validated by a program (CMVP). Exercises cross-document conformance (FIPS -> ISO 19790, modified by SP 800-140x) and shows requirement granularity varies by source (tmodel #5/#15). Measures how poisoning susceptibility grows with model size across 24 open models, and shows 2% disguised poison passing OpenAI's fine-tuning moderation; AIT-017 and AIT-081 cite it and NIST AI 100-2e2025 cites it as [46]. Poisoning and Backdooring Contrastive Learning
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
By its own account the first poisoning and backdoor attacks on multimodal contrastive training: 3 poisoned pairs in 3 million give 40% targeted success and 0.01% gives 50% backdoor success. AIT-013 cites it. Wild Patterns Reloaded: A Survey of Machine Learning Security against Training Data Poisoning
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
A systematization of poisoning attacks and defenses with a threat-modeling framework, a unified bilevel formulation and an attack-defense matching; NIST AI 100-2e2025 bases its poisoning classification on it and AIT-011 cites it. paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
The reference taxonomy for harmful fine-tuning attacks and the 100+ proposed defences, with an explicit fine-tuning-as-a-service threat model; a survey whose authors are also the authors of several surveyed defences. Backdoor Learning: A Survey
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written

paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written

evasion

"Real Attackers Don't Compute Gradients": Bridging the Gap Between Adversarial ML Research and Practice
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Practitioner case studies of real ML evasion plus a systematic review of 88 papers; argues for system-wide, cost-driven threat models. Primary in-the-wild evidence is a 100-sample judgement study, and the paper concedes it cannot prove the absence of gradient-based attacks. Reliable evaluation of adversarial robustness with an ensemble of diverse parameter-free attacks
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
The standard parameter-free test for evasion robustness of image classifiers. Its evaluation of 58 models found that most published defences overstated their robustness, which is evidence about how far mitigation claims can be trusted. A Survey of Attacks on Large Vision-Language Models: Resources, Advances, and Future Trends
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A broad survey of attacks on vision-language models; useful as a map of multimodal attack classes, less so for agentic/MCP specifics. Adversarial Machine Learning Attacks and Defense Methods in the Cyber Security Domain
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
A structured review of attacks on ML-based security products, with per-attack feasibility marks (Tables 1-7) and a taxonomy of attacker knowledge and access; AIT-007 cites it. Accessorize to a Crime: Real and Stealthy Attacks on State-of-the-Art Face Recognition
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Primary demonstration of physically realizable, inconspicuous evasion and impersonation of face recognition (white-box, plus a black-box commercial API); small, controlled subject set. Intriguing properties of neural networks
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Primary source for adversarial examples and their transfer across models and training sets; the evidence base for CWE-1039 (its REF-16) and NIST's evasion class.

CSL-JSON is the interchange format docs/construction.md selected over BibTeX; this repo is its normative schema. SD Elements — Exporting an OTM from Devici

jailbreak

Scaling Trends for Data Poisoning in LLMs
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-supply-chain-infra · summarized · summary written
Measures how poisoning susceptibility grows with model size across 24 open models, and shows 2% disguised poison passing OpenAI's fine-tuning moderation; AIT-017 and AIT-081 cite it and NIST AI 100-2e2025 cites it as [46]. Harmful Fine-tuning Attacks and Defenses for Large Language Models: A Survey
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
The reference taxonomy for harmful fine-tuning attacks and the 100+ proposed defences, with an explicit fine-tuning-as-a-service threat model; a survey whose authors are also the authors of several surveyed defences. AdvWave: Stealthy Adversarial Jailbreak Attack against Large Audio-Language Models
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Primary white-box jailbreak attack on audio-language models, with a dual-phase method that works around non-differentiable audio tokenizers; digital audio only, three open models. Malla: Demystifying Real-world Large Language Model Integrated Malicious Services
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
The first measurement of real underground LLM-based malicious services (14 services, 198 projects): their backends, jailbreak prompts, prices and output quality. Primary evidence of jailbreak and uncensored-model misuse at scale. MM-SafetyBench: A Benchmark for Safety Evaluation of Multimodal Large Language Models
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
A 5,040-pair benchmark showing that query-relevant images, especially typography, raise jailbreak success on 12 open multimodal models; ASR is judged by GPT-4. A Survey of Attacks on Large Vision-Language Models: Resources, Advances, and Future Trends
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A broad survey of attacks on vision-language models; useful as a map of multimodal attack classes, less so for agentic/MCP specifics. JailBreakV: A Benchmark for Assessing the Robustness of MultiModal Large Language Models against Jailbreak Attacks
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
A 28,000-case benchmark showing that text jailbreaks written for LLMs transfer to multimodal LLMs regardless of the image; judged by Llama Guard on 10 open models. Ignore This Title and HackAPrompt: Exposing Systemic Vulnerabilities of LLMs through a Global Scale Prompt Hacking Competition
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A 600K-prompt human attack dataset and a taxonomy of prompt-hacking techniques; useful evidence for direct injection and jailbreaks, less for agents. Mitigating Skeleton Key, a new type of generative AI jailbreak technique
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Vendor disclosure of a guideline-augmentation jailbreak that worked on seven major models in April-May 2024, with Microsoft's mitigations; no success rates published. Deceptive Delight: Jailbreak LLMs Through Camouflage and Distraction
article · vendor · topic ai-threat-model-ml-risk-assets · summarized · summary written
Vendor research on a two-to-three-turn jailbreak that hides an unsafe topic among benign ones; 64.6% average success over 8,000 cases on eight anonymized models with content filters disabled.

key-management

Proof-of-Possession Key Semantics for CBOR Web Tokens (CWTs)
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
PoP confirmation claim for CWT. Prior art for binding a token to a key; not required by the current envelope options. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE): Initial Algorithms
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · summarized · summary written
The algorithm half of COSE. Updated by RFC 9864 — read them together or you will implement under-specified algorithm identifiers. Ephemeral Diffie-Hellman Over COSE (EDHOC)
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-ecosystem · fetched · summary not written
A key exchange over COSE for constrained devices. Ecosystem evidence — m-of-n does not do key agreement. Traces of Ephemeral Diffie-Hellman Over COSE (EDHOC)
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-ecosystem · fetched · summary not written
Test vectors for EDHOC, which we do not implement. Recorded to close the question. Using Ephemeral Diffie-Hellman Over COSE (EDHOC) with the Constrained Application Protocol (CoAP) and Object Security for Constrained RESTful Environments (OSCORE)
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-ecosystem · fetched · summary not written
EDHOC/CoAP integration. Outside m-of-n's transport story; recorded to close the question. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE) Key Thumbprint
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
DEC-003 lists 'COSE key thumbprint' as an option and requires canonical key bytes before hashing. This is that specification. Fully-Specified Algorithms for JSON Object Signing and Encryption (JOSE) and CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE)
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
Removes polymorphic algorithm identifiers. Anything implementing RFC 9053 today must apply this. Secure Hash Standard (SHS)
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Defines the SHA-2 family. A principal named by key digest under R-M-02 is named by one of these functions, so which are approved is load-bearing on that naming. Digital Signature Standard (DSS)
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Defines the signature schemes a key-centric model will actually use; supplies publisher test vectors for PROC-0002 stage 8. SHA-3 Standard: Permutation-Based Hash and Extendable-Output Functions
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Defines SHA-3 and the SHAKE extendable-output functions. It does not replace FIPS 180-4 — both are approved, which is itself the finding a key-digest naming scheme has to resolve. Module-Lattice-Based Key-Encapsulation Mechanism Standard
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
ML-KEM is key ENCAPSULATION - confidentiality, not authentication. Nothing in a key-centric statement model turns on it. Held as the post-quantum counterpart to the signature records, not because it bears on a requirement. Recommendations for Discrete Logarithm-based Cryptography: Elliptic Curve Domain Parameters
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Fixes the curve set a federal-facing ECDSA or EdDSA key may come from. A principal named by key or key digest under R-M-02 is named by a point on one of these curves, so which curves are approved bounds that naming — even though ARCH-0001 NG3 defers the suite choice itself. Recommendation for Key Management: Part 1 - General
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Supplies the key-lifecycle vocabulary - cryptoperiod, key state, algorithm strength, transition - that a model naming principals by key needs in order to say what happens when a key expires. We conform to no clause in it. Post-Quantum Key Encapsulation Mechanisms (PQ KEMs) for COSE
draft · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
Key encapsulation is confidentiality, not attestation - the same negative verdict recorded on fips-203. Held so the post-quantum COSE picture is complete, and because the WG rename makes it easy to miss. JSON Web Algorithms (JWA)
rfc · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
The JOSE half of the algorithm registry. COSE's design is legible as a set of deliberate departures from it, so holding both is what makes the comparison possible. Recommendation for Block Cipher Modes of Operation: Methods for Key Wrapping
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
AES Key Wrap is the KW half of JOSE A128KW/A192KW/A256KW and of every ML-KEM+AxxxKW composite in draft-ietf-jose-pqc-kem. #8 lists it as ingest-if-needed; the post-quantum KEM draft is the path that needs it. Recommendation for Pair-Wise Key-Establishment Schemes Using Discrete Logarithm Cryptography
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Named in SP 800-186 Table 2 as the authority for the allowed key-establishment usage of P-224/256/384/521. The library cited that table without holding the document it points at.

post-quantum

Module-Lattice-Based Key-Encapsulation Mechanism Standard
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
ML-KEM is key ENCAPSULATION - confidentiality, not authentication. Nothing in a key-centric statement model turns on it. Held as the post-quantum counterpart to the signature records, not because it bears on a requirement. Module-Lattice-Based Digital Signature Standard
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
The NIST post-quantum signature standard most likely to be the default. Its key and signature sizes are what a key-centric statement model would actually carry, and its 255-byte context string is a domain-separation hook that lands near P4. Stateless Hash-Based Digital Signature Standard
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
The conservative post-quantum signature fallback: its security rests only on hash functions, with no lattice assumption. Held as the hedge against ML-DSA, and as the record that shows what signature size a hash-based scheme actually costs. Recommendation for Stateful Hash-Based Signature Schemes
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Approves LMS/HSS and XMSS/XMSS-MT - signature schemes whose security rests only on the hash function, and whose STATEFULNESS makes key reuse a correctness failure rather than a weakness. That constraint bears directly on signing canonical bytes. SLH-DSA for JOSE and COSE
draft · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
The only wire binding for SLH-DSA. Answers what fips-205 could not: what an SLH-DSA signature looks like in COSE - and that only two of twelve parameter sets are registered. Post-Quantum Key Encapsulation Mechanisms (PQ KEMs) for COSE
draft · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
Key encapsulation is confidentiality, not attestation - the same negative verdict recorded on fips-203. Held so the post-quantum COSE picture is complete, and because the WG rename makes it easy to miss. Internet X.509 Public Key Infrastructure — Algorithm Identifiers for the Module-Lattice-Based Digital Signature Algorithm (ML-DSA)
rfc · ietf · topic algorithm-bindings · summarized · summary written
Holds the argument RFC 9964 7.2 defers to. Without it the reason COSE excludes HashML-DSA was a citation the library could not check.

A secondary case study of the attack RPT-0009 models step by step (em-01), written from public sources; its account of the bypass chain is the evidence for gaps G1 to G7. Indirect Prompt Injection in the Wild: An Empirical Study of Prevalence, Techniques, and Objectives
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
First large-scale measurement of indirect prompt injection on the real web: shows the threat is already deployed, mostly hidden in machine-only channels, with measured model compliance. A Survey of Attacks on Large Vision-Language Models: Resources, Advances, and Future Trends

A broad survey of attacks on vision-language models; useful as a map of multimodal attack classes, less so for agentic/MCP specifics. SkillSecurer: Detecting and Patching Prompt-Injection Vulnerabilities in AI Agent Skills
paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
Measures prompt-injection risk in agent skills (reusable instruction/script packages) with a red/blue agent framework; finds latent vulnerabilities in over 17% of popular public skills. Lessons from Penetration Tests on Large-Scale Agent Systems
paper · academic · topic sagai · stub · summary written
Field evidence from two 2025 penetration tests of proprietary agents: indirect prompt injection led to unintended tool use and remote code execution, and user approval was not a reliable boundary. paper · academic · topic ai-threat-model-agentic-mcp · summarized · summary written
A 600K-prompt human attack dataset and a taxonomy of prompt-hacking techniques; useful evidence for direct injection and jailbreaks, less for agents. Tensor Trust: Interpretable Prompt Injection Attacks from an Online Game

Algorithm profile. ARCH-0001 NG3 defers the algorithm suite, so this is needed only if RSA is later admitted. CBOR Web Token (CWT)
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
ARCH-0001 Sec.6 names COSE_Sign1 + CWT as an envelope candidate and asks that iss/sub align with the KeyId profile. Use of the HSS/LMS Hash-Based Signature Algorithm with CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE)
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
Stateful hash-based signatures in COSE. Algorithm suite is deferred by NG3; relevant if post-quantum signing is admitted. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE) and JSON Object Signing and Encryption (JOSE) Registrations for Web Authentication (WebAuthn) Algorithms
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-ecosystem · fetched · summary not written
The WebAuthn/COSE algorithm bridge. Evidence for the FIDO half of the ecosystem survey, not for our envelope. Use of the Walnut Digital Signature Algorithm with CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE)

Informational registration for an algorithm outside any suite m-of-n would consider. Recorded so it is not re-evaluated. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE): Structures and Process

STD 96 with RFC 9338. COSE_Sign1 is one of the two envelope options in DEC-005, and its header parameters are IANA-allocated, so R-M-12 applies. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE): Initial Algorithms
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · summarized · summary written
The algorithm half of COSE. Updated by RFC 9864 — read them together or you will implement under-specified algorithm identifiers. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE): Hash Algorithms
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
R-M-07 requires artifact statements to bind a content digest; this registers the COSE hash algorithm identifiers for doing so. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE): Countersignatures
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
Part of STD 96 with RFC 9052. Countersignature is the closest COSE primitive to an endorsement over someone else's statement. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE): Header Parameters for Carrying and Referencing X.509 Certificates
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
X.509 carriage is the identifier model m-of-n is arguing against, but DEC-003 must say why, and interop may need it. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE): AES-CTR and AES-CBC

Content encryption modes. m-of-n signs rather than encrypts; kept for completeness of the COSE algorithm picture. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE) "typ" (type) Header Parameter
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
Explicit typing of a signed object — the standard defence against cross-protocol confusion between statement kinds. CBOR Web Token (CWT) Claims in COSE Headers
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
Puts CWT claims in the protected header, so a verifier reads issuer and subject without parsing the payload. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE) Key Thumbprint
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
DEC-003 lists 'COSE key thumbprint' as an option and requires canonical key bytes before hashing. This is that specification. Fully-Specified Algorithms for JSON Object Signing and Encryption (JOSE) and CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE)

Removes polymorphic algorithm identifiers. Anything implementing RFC 9053 today must apply this. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE) Header Parameter for Timestamp Tokens as Defined in RFC 3161
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
Carries an RFC 3161 timestamp in a COSE header. Relevant only if we need third-party time attestation. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE) Receipts

The receipt format the SCITT architecture (RFC 9943, already held) relies on. Directly relevant to the transparency story. ML-DSA for JSON Object Signing and Encryption (JOSE) and CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE)
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · summarized · summary written
The current post-quantum signature binding for COSE, and the concrete mechanism behind R-M-02 for PQ keys: its AKP thumbprint rules (6) fix exactly which parameters enter a key digest. Upgraded from marginal on 2026-10-03 after reading 6 against rfc-9679. CBOR Object Signing and Encryption (COSE) Hash Envelope
rfc · ietf · topic cbor-implementation · fetched · summary not written
Signing over a detached payload digest — the pattern R-M-07 describes for artifact statements that must not carry the artifact. Digital Signature Standard (DSS), FIPS 186-4
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · stub · summary not written Digital Signature Standard (DSS)
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Defines the signature schemes a key-centric model will actually use; supplies publisher test vectors for PROC-0002 stage 8. Module-Lattice-Based Digital Signature Standard
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
The NIST post-quantum signature standard most likely to be the default. Its key and signature sizes are what a key-centric statement model would actually carry, and its 255-byte context string is a domain-separation hook that lands near P4. Stateless Hash-Based Digital Signature Standard
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
The conservative post-quantum signature fallback: its security rests only on hash functions, with no lattice assumption. Held as the hedge against ML-DSA, and as the record that shows what signature size a hash-based scheme actually costs. Recommendations for Discrete Logarithm-based Cryptography: Elliptic Curve Domain Parameters
spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
Fixes the curve set a federal-facing ECDSA or EdDSA key may come from. A principal named by key or key digest under R-M-02 is named by a point on one of these curves, so which curves are approved bounds that naming — even though ARCH-0001 NG3 defers the suite choice itself. spec · nist · topic nist-cryptography · summarized · summary written
